# Handy baseline license and immutable-artifact audit

Research answer for **Verify licenses and immutable artifact access for the
Handy baseline**, audited on 2026-07-19. This follows the exact baseline fixed by
[Handy catalog parity baseline](handy-catalog-parity-baseline.md): Handy commit
[`cdbc22390987643237756382ef367f7244b2844f`](https://github.com/cjpais/Handy/commit/cdbc22390987643237756382ef367f7244b2844f),
catalog generated at `2026-07-01T13:30:59Z`, 65 entries and 337 GGUF files.

This is an engineering planning and risk assessment, **not legal advice**. A
license identifier in a model card is evidence of the publisher's claim, not a
chain-of-title opinion. Release counsel should approve any hosted redistribution
and every unresolved/custom-license flow.

## Executive answer

The immutable technical inventory is complete. Each of the 65 public
`handy-computer` conversion repositories has been resolved to the last Hugging
Face commit at or before the catalog timestamp. Every one of the 337 catalog
files has revision-specific LFS metadata: **337 hashes present, zero filename or
byte-size mismatches**. The exact revisions and hashes are in the appendices.
Handy's moving `resolve/main/...` URLs should therefore be replaced with the
appendix's `resolve/{revision}/...` identities if FoldWise means the pinned
baseline ([Handy's pinned runtime uses `main`](https://github.com/cjpais/Handy/blob/cdbc22390987643237756382ef367f7244b2844f/src-tauri/src/catalog/mod.rs),
[Hugging Face revision/blob API pattern](https://huggingface.co/docs/hub/api)).

Legal parity is not equally complete:

- All 65 GGUF conversion repositories are public and ungated at their pinned
  revisions. The **original** Cohere Transcribe and Google MedASR repositories
  are gated (`gated: auto`): their public pages require login/sign-up, sharing
  contact information, and accepting conditions. Their Handy GGUF conversions
  are nevertheless public. The gate and the converted artifact's access must
  be shown as separate facts ([Cohere public gate](https://huggingface.co/CohereLabs/cohere-transcribe-03-2026),
  [MedASR public gate](https://huggingface.co/google/medasr)).
- Six localized Moonshine Tiny originals declare only `other` and six localized
  Moonshine Base originals provide no license metadata, while all twelve Handy
  cards say MIT. No pinned original card supplies authority for that apparent
  relicensing. Those twelve entries remain **license unresolved** and should not
  be enabled for download, cache, or redistribution without licensor
  confirmation.
- `nvidia/canary-1b` and its conversion are CC-BY-NC-4.0. Catalog display is
  possible, but a commercial product's facilitation, caching, or redistribution
  is a fact-sensitive NonCommercial question; it cannot support unconditional
  one-click parity ([CC-BY-NC-4.0 legal code](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc/4.0/legalcode.en)).
- MedASR is governed by Google HAI-DEF terms, not a generic open-source license.
  Distribution includes hosted service and requires downstream enforceable use
  restrictions, agreement delivery, notice, modification marking, and regulatory
  authorization where applicable ([HAI-DEF terms, sections 1 and 3](https://developers.google.com/health-ai-developer-foundations/terms)).
- Other custom originals require their own compliance: NVIDIA Open Model License
  (`parakeet-unified`, `nemotron-speech-streaming`), OpenMDW-1.1
  (`nemotron-3.5-asr-streaming`), and FunASR Model License 1.1
  (`SenseVoiceSmall`). A conversion card's `other`, CC-BY, or MIT label does not
  replace those upstream terms.

FoldWise can truthfully promise **catalog-inventory parity**: all 65 entries may
be represented, with legal/access status. It cannot yet promise that every row
is universally downloadable in-app, redistributable by FoldWise, or usable in
every commercial context. Product copy must keep these promises separate.

The 16 conversion/original metadata discrepancies are exactly: one
`parakeet-unified` conversion (CC-BY-4.0 versus NVIDIA Open Model License), two
Fun-ASR Nano conversions (`other` versus Apache-2.0), twelve localized Moonshine
conversions (MIT versus six `other` and six missing originals), and Whisper
large-v3-turbo (Apache-2.0 versus MIT). The immutable cards are linked in their
matrix rows below. These are not automatically license violations; they are
proof that the conversion label cannot be FoldWise's only compliance input.

## Product effects by surface

| Surface | Planning conclusion |
| --- | --- |
| Catalog visibility | Keep all 65 entries visible as the Handy baseline, but label original access and legal status separately. Mark the 12 localized Moonshine entries `license unresolved`, MedASR `terms acceptance required`, Cohere/MedASR `original gated`, and Canary 1B `noncommercial`. Do not translate public conversion access into a claim that the original is ungated or that conversion was authorized. |
| Direct in-app Hugging Face download | Technically possible for all 65 pinned conversion repositories because they are public. Enable only where the app can present and retain the applicable license/attribution record. Require explicit terms handling for custom licenses; hold the 12 unresolved Moonshine entries; obtain legal review before enabling Canary 1B in a commercial app. Cohere's original gate does not technically block its public conversion, but the exact original click-through conditions remain unreviewed. |
| FoldWise-hosted redistribution, bundling, or persistent mirror | This is the highest-duty path. Ship a per-artifact notice manifest, applicable license text, origin/source, modification/conversion statement, and upstream notices. HAI-DEF additionally requires enforceable downstream restrictions and its exact Notice; NVIDIA requires its agreement and exact Notice; Moonshine Community, if confirmed applicable, requires its agreement, exact Notice, and prominent “Powered by Moonshine AI”; CC-BY-NC needs commercial-use review. Do not host the 12 unresolved Moonshine conversions. |
| Complete-parity promise | Safe only when qualified as **inventory parity with Handy's pinned catalog**. “Every model downloadable” or “full usable parity” is false until legal gates are implemented and unresolved entries cleared. Availability, selection, and effective use remain distinct in FoldWise's domain and lifecycle ([CONTEXT](../../CONTEXT.md), [ADR-0005](../adr/0005-second-asr-engine-behind-transcribing.md), [ADR-0006](../adr/0006-global-asr-selection-over-revived-asr-model.md)). |

This separation fits the existing architecture: the ASR lifecycle owns catalog
resolution and adapter-validated availability, optional downloads are
storage-only, and selection is transactional. A legal/access state should be an
input to the catalog/download policy; it must not be inferred from local
directory presence or silently change selection
([ADR-0005](../adr/0005-second-asr-engine-behind-transcribing.md),
[ADR-0006](../adr/0006-global-asr-selection-over-revived-asr-model.md)).
Pipeline remains insulated behind its opaque session handle
([ADR-0002](../adr/0002-pipeline-hybrid-seams.md)).

## Duty classes

These are minimum planning controls. “Original” and “conversion card” may name
different licenses; FoldWise should preserve the stricter/applicable provenance
layers until counsel confirms which terms govern the conversion.

| Code | Primary terms | Minimum planning duties and constraints |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `A2` | [Apache-2.0](https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0.txt) | Give recipients the license; retain copyright, patent, trademark, and attribution notices; mark modified files; reproduce applicable NOTICE content. Patent-litigation termination and no trademark grant remain. |
| `MIT` | [MIT text in the pinned Whisper upstream](https://github.com/openai/whisper/blob/6e3be77e1a105e59086e3e21ff5f609fd6fa89a5/LICENSE) | Include copyright and permission notice in all copies or substantial portions. |
| `BY` | [CC-BY-4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/legalcode.en) | Preserve supplied creator/source/copyright/license/disclaimer information, link the license, and indicate modifications; do not impose effective downstream restrictions. Commercial use is allowed. |
| `BY-NC` | [CC-BY-NC-4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc/4.0/legalcode.en) | Same attribution/change duties as `BY`, but licensed rights are NonCommercial only. Canary 1B needs a commercial-product decision before download or redistribution. |
| `NVIDIA` | [NVIDIA Open Model License](https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/agreements/enterprise-software/nvidia-open-model-license/) | On distribution, provide the agreement and a Notice file stating “Licensed by NVIDIA Corporation under the NVIDIA Open Model License”; preserve separate-component terms. Trustworthy-AI, guardrail, special-purpose, export/sanctions, litigation-termination, and indemnity terms also apply. |
| `OMDW` | [OpenMDW-1.1](https://openmdw.ai/license/1-1/) | Retain the agreement and every applicable copyright/origin notice when distributing Model Materials; specified IP litigation terminates rights. |
| `FUN` | [FunASR Model License 1.1 at the revision linked by the SenseVoice card](https://github.com/modelscope/FunASR/blob/58830eca4012644aac0c3218c3ccc7d98f003fda/MODEL_LICENSE) | Attribute source and author and retain relevant model names for use/copy/modification/sharing. The text has conduct restrictions, automatic termination, automatic future revisions, and an unresolved governing-law placeholder. |
| `HAI` | [Google HAI-DEF terms](https://developers.google.com/health-ai-developer-foundations/terms) | Pass Section 3.2 restrictions as enforceable downstream terms; notify users and give them the agreement; seek health authorization when applicable; mark changes; for non-hosted distribution include the exact HAI-DEF Notice. Hosted service is expressly “Distribution.” Original HF access is gated. |
| `MOON?` | [Pinned Tiny example (`other`)](https://huggingface.co/UsefulSensors/moonshine-tiny-vi/blob/d4d20dada16b64c94ca9e1c765fc4d3d968d496e/README.md), [pinned Base example (“More Information Needed”)](https://huggingface.co/UsefulSensors/moonshine-base-ar/blob/264cc18a24b978dc5a150396663e4434391a59af/README.md) | No reliable minimum can be derived from the pinned originals. The publisher's current [Moonshine Community License](https://www.moonshine.ai/moonshine_community_license.txt) has revenue thresholds, registration, attribution/Notice and “Powered by” duties, but the pinned cards do not incorporate it. Hold download/redistribution until the licensor confirms the governing text and the converter's authority. |

## Per-entry model, license, and access matrix

Each revision link is immutable. `Conv` is the Handy conversion-card license;
`Original` is the pinned upstream card's license/name. `Gate` always reports the
**original** gate followed by the conversion gate, so `auto/public` never means
the conversion itself is gated. `Duties` are the codes above. `Unresolved`
means the legal chain is incomplete, not that the artifact hash is unknown.

## Evidence method and remaining uncertainty

For each conversion entry, the audit selected the newest Hugging Face commit
whose timestamp was not later than `2026-07-01T13:30:59Z`, then queried that
immutable revision with `?blobs=true`. It compared every catalog filename and
byte count with the returned sibling record and recorded
`siblings[].lfs.sha256`. Public original repositories were resolved the same
way. Cohere and Google gate their original commit/revision APIs; for those two,
the audit used the public top-level SHA only because its `lastModified` was
already before the cutoff, and records that lower-confidence resolution instead
of pretending the gated history was inspected. The 65-row matrix links each
conversion card, original card, and revision API directly. The pinned Handy
generator establishes that license/base-model values originate in conversion
cards, which is why independent upstream verification is necessary
([generator](https://github.com/cjpais/Handy/blob/cdbc22390987643237756382ef367f7244b2844f/scripts/gen_catalog.py)).

Remaining uncertainties are legal/provenance questions, not missing hashes:

1. The twelve localized Moonshine conversions lack a verified upstream license
   chain; current publisher terms cannot be retroactively assumed to govern the
   pinned originals.
2. The exact Cohere and Google Hugging Face click-through forms could not be
   read without authenticated acceptance. Their public cards establish the gate
   and public license/terms labels, but counsel should review the accepted form.
3. No independent conversion license file or converter chain-of-title was found
   for the 65 GGUF repositories; card metadata alone is not such a document.
4. Model repositories can contain separately licensed tokenizer, vocabulary,
   code, dataset, or third-party components. Before FoldWise mirrors artifacts,
   build a file-level NOTICE inventory rather than treating a one-word card
   license as exhaustive.
5. The NVIDIA, FunASR, Moonshine, and Google terms are mutable web documents.
   Store the reviewed text/version with release provenance; do not rely on a
   moving URL as the only compliance record.
